# Appendix<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002258650632"></a>

<!-- md-trans-meta sourceCommit=unknown translatedAt=2026-09-07T02:03:18.571Z -->

>[!NOTE]Note
> 
>API parameters are classified into optional and required parameters. However, DBMind sets default values for some required parameters. After receiving a user request, the DBMind backend first validates the parameters, including checking whether any required parameter is not passed. Therefore, if a parameter is required, has no default value, and is not passed by the user, the request reports an error during parameter validation.

After the DBMind process is started, the HTTP\(S\) APIs it provides are as follows:

**Table 1**  DBMind RESTful API list

<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_table117171681772"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row139482081377"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.1"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1194898678"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1194898678"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1194898678"></a>API</p></th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.2"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p79481881376"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p79481881376"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p79481881376"></a>Request Method</p></th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.3"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1794868479"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1794868479"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1794868479"></a>Description</p></th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.4"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p129481782716"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p129481782716"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p129481782716"></a>Parameter</p></th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.5"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p7948138572"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p7948138572"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p7948138572"></a>Return Value</p></th>
<th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" id="mcps1.2.7.1.6"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p094818814713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p094818814713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p094818814713"></a>Example</p></th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row7948089714"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19481081871"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19481081871"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19481081871"></a>/v1/api/token</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199481981879"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199481981879"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199481981879"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p694817811718"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p694817811718"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p694817811718"></a>Obtains the database access token.</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note149375293818"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note149375293818"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note149375293818"></a><span class="notetitle">Note: </span><div class="notebody"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6937329180"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6937329180"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6937329180"></a>When the RPC service (opengauss_exporter) is started normally and a login exception occurs, such as incorrect user credentials or a locked user, the API returns the error message returned by the database. Therefore, if this API is used for brute-force attacks, the user is locked after the number of failed logins exceeds the failed_login_attempts value configured in the database.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="notice" id="note2074871320212"><a name="note2074871320212"></a><a name="note2074871320212"></a><span class="noticetitle">Caution: </span><div class="noticebody"><a name="ul236161816238"></a><a name="ul236161816238"></a><ul id="ul236161816238"><li>Users who use DBMind must have the monitor admin privilege. Therefore, the user role is checked when a user obtains a token.</li><li>When obtaining a token, do not use the same user as the opengauss-exporter collection user. The reasons are as follows:<a name="ul2937131472511"></a><a name="ul2937131472511"></a><ul id="ul2937131472511"><li>The token API has a brute-force prevention mechanism. If the number of failed logins exceeds the failed_login_attempts value configured in the kernel, the database user is locked. Because opengauss-exporter periodically connects to the instance to collect metrics, the number of failed token requests made with this user may be reset by the successful connections of opengauss-exporter, which may prevent the user from being locked normally.</li><li>The opengauss-exporter user should be a system user for machine-to-machine interfaces, while the DBMind token user should be a separately created DBMind human-machine interface user.</li></ul>
</li></ul>
<p id="p161129102317"><a name="p161129102317"></a><a name="p161129102317"></a></p>
</div></div></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul16895162104619"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul16895162104619"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul16895162104619"><li>param username: Required. User name.</li><li>param grant_type: Optional. Authorization type.</li><li>param password: Required. Password.</li><li>param scope: Required. Database instance.</li><li>param client_id: Optional. Client ID.</li><li>param client_secret: Optional. Client secret.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1094898870"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1094898870"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1094898870"></a>Returns the token.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12948284713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12948284713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12948284713"></a>{"access_token":"qmWWgt28VSu0YAH5","token_type":"bearer","expires_in":600}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row11948981074"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19948781073"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19948781073"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p19948781073"></a>/v1/api/agents</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1694888874"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1694888874"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1694888874"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p394814820717"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p394814820717"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p394814820717"></a>Obtains the list of all agents.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p59481082717"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p59481082717"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p59481082717"></a>None</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1594848677"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1594848677"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1594848677"></a>Returns the list of all agents.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1394817818719"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1394817818719"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1394817818719"></a>{"data":{"ip1":["ip1","ip2","ip3"]}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row2948989713"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17948081078"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17948081078"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17948081078"></a>/v1/api/agents</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89495812713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89495812713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89495812713"></a>PUT</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17880164495711"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17880164495711"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p17880164495711"></a>Updates agent information (including forced update).</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note41871815121914"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note41871815121914"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note41871815121914"></a><span class="notetitle">Note: </span><div class="notebody"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11187715171917"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11187715171917"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11187715171917"></a>Because changing the database instance status takes some time, forced update is delayed by 15 seconds by default.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p18472193118199"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p18472193118199"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p18472193118199"></a>The update does not block other APIs. However, because the agent information is not updated yet, the information obtained during the wait may be the old information before the update.</p>
</div></div></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109492081371"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109492081371"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109492081371"></a>param force: Optional. Forced update.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p15949683711"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p15949683711"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p15949683711"></a>Returns the agent update result.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12949138774"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12949138774"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p12949138774"></a>{"data":true,"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row129518819714"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139511581575"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139511581575"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139511581575"></a>/v1/api/app/slow-sql-rca</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1395118272"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1395118272"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1395118272"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p195118675"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p195118675"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p195118675"></a>Obtains the root cause analysis result of slow SQL.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1952745104715"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1952745104715"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1952745104715"><li>param query: Required. SQL query statement. The string length must be less than 10240.</li><li>param db_name: Required. Database name.</li><li>param schema_name: Optional. Schema name. Default value: public.</li><li>param start_time: Optional. 13-digit timestamp. Recommended. Start time.</li><li>param finish_time: Optional. 13-digit timestamp. Recommended. End time.</li><li>param template_id: Optional. Recommended. Normalized SQL ID.</li><li>param debug_query_id: Optional. Recommended. Unique SQL ID.</li><li>param n_soft_parse: Optional. Number of soft parses.</li><li>param n_hard_parse: Optional. Number of hard parses.</li><li>param query_plan: Recommended. Execution plan.</li><li>param n_returned_rows: Optional. Number of tuples in the result.</li><li>param n_tuples_fetched: Optional. Number of randomly scanned rows.</li><li>param n_tuples_returned: Optional. Number of sequentially scanned rows.</li><li>param n_tuples_inserted: Optional. Number of inserted rows.</li><li>param n_tuples_updated: Optional. Number of updated rows.</li><li>param n_tuples_deleted: Optional. Number of deleted rows.</li><li>param n_blocks_fetched: Optional. Number of buffer block accesses.</li><li>param n_blocks_hit: Optional. Number of buffer block hits.</li><li>param db_time: Optional. Effective DB time spent, in microseconds.</li><li>param cpu_time: Optional. CPU time, in microseconds.</li><li>param parse_time: Optional. Parse time, in microseconds.</li><li>param plan_time: Optional. Execution time, in microseconds.</li><li>param data_io_time: Optional. I/O time, in microseconds.</li><li>param hash_spill_count: Optional. Number of file writes if a spill to disk during hashing.</li><li>param sort_spill_count: Optional. Number of file writes if a fallback occurs during sorting.</li><li>param n_calls: Optional. Number of calls.</li><li>param lock_wait_time: Optional. Lock wait time.</li><li>param lwlock_wait_time: Optional. Lightweight lock time.</li><li>param tz: Optional. Recommended if there is a time zone difference. Time zone information of slow SQL. Only the UTC standard is supported.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1895217818714"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1895217818714"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1895217818714"></a>Returns the root cause analysis result of slow SQL.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p101087149418"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p101087149418"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p101087149418"></a>{"data":[true,"public","Seq Scan on t2  (cost=0.00..3.25 rows=1 width=70)\n  Filter: (c1 = 'dadsadasds'::text)\n",[[["1. MISSING_INDEXES: (0.53) Missing required index.","2. HEAVY_SCAN_OPERATOR: (0.47) Existing expensive seq scans. Detail: (name: Seq Scan on t2, parent: None, rows:t2(100), cost rate: 100.0%)"]],[["1. Recommended index: (schema: public, index: t2(c1)).","2. According to business adjustments, try to avoid it"]]]],"success":true}</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note81765187357"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note81765187357"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note81765187357"></a><span class="notetitle">Note: </span><div class="notebody"><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10329257185016"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10329257185016"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10329257185016"></a>For details, see the precautions.</p>
<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul161301225154618"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul161301225154618"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul161301225154618"><li>When a normalized QUERY statement is passed in, the execution plan is obtained internally by using the PBE method. If the retrieval fails, the execution plan content is not returned.</li><li>If the user performing the diagnosis has insufficient privileges, the result cannot be returned normally.</li><li>The API response time is affected by the database load and resources.</li><li>It is recommended to pass in the query_plan parameter to avoid internal retrieval.</li><li>If the passed QUERY is truncated and the query_plan parameter is not passed in, the diagnosis cannot be performed.</li><li>The API supports only one QUERY statement. If multiple statements are passed in, only the first QUERY is diagnosed.</li><li>If the schema information is not passed in, the schema defaults to PUBLIC.</li><li>Currently, only DML statements are supported.</li></ul>
</div></div></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row169528811719"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952882716"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952882716"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952882716"></a>/v1/api/app/risk-analysis/{metric}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p795219810713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p795219810713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p795219810713"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p149524814717"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p149524814717"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p149524814717"></a>Obtains the risk analysis result.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1530772116414"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1530772116414"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1530772116414"><li>param metric: Required. Metric name.</li><li>param instance: Required. Instance address.</li><li>param warning_hours: Optional. Risk analysis duration.</li><li>param upper: Optional. Upper limit of the metric.</li><li>param lower: Optional. Lower limit of the metric.</li><li>param labels: Optional. Labels of the metric. Supports the following two formats:<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1220115781417"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1220115781417"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul1220115781417"><li>The kv pairs format of "k1=v1,k2=v2";</li><li>The JSON string format of "{'k1':'v1','k2':'v2'}";</li></ul>
</li><li>param tz: Optional. Time zone parameter. Only the UTC standard is supported.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952581172"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952581172"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p13952581172"></a>Returns the future risk analysis result of the current metric.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p159521682713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p159521682713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p159521682713"></a>{"data":{},"success":true}</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note630805181718"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note630805181718"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note630805181718"></a><span class="notetitle">Note: </span><div class="notebody"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ol128224152513"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ol128224152513"></a><ol id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ol128224152513"><li>This API predicts the future trend of metrics based on historical data. During prediction, it uses historical data three times the length of warning_hours as training data to predict the future trend of the metric. Therefore, if warning_hours is too small and the historical data is insufficient, the training data may be too little to perform training, and no normal result will be output. In addition, prediction results for 48 hours or longer may be inaccurate.</li><li>This API is suitable for predicting data with trends and periodic changes.</li><li>When the labels parameter is not passed to this API for filtering, the API may time out if too many metrics meet the conditions.</li><li>This prediction function is based on an AI model, so each prediction result may differ slightly.</li></ol>
</div></div></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row10952888714"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16952481677"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16952481677"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16952481677"></a>/v1/api/app/anomaly-detection/detectors/{name}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p09521181671"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p09521181671"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p09521181671"></a>PUT</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p119521286717"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p119521286717"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p119521286717"></a>Adds a new anomaly detection detector or modifies the current detector.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p55742920528"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p55742920528"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p55742920528"></a>param name: Required. Detector name.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3490132993518"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3490132993518"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3490132993518"></a>The following JSON structure must be passed in:</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p05732935215"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p05732935215"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p05732935215"></a>{</p>

<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p25732917529"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p25732917529"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p25732917529"></a>"forecasting_seconds": 0,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1571629185217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1571629185217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1571629185217"></a>"alarm_info": {</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1157152912523"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1157152912523"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1157152912523"></a>"alarm_type": "SYSTEM",</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p5571729165210"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p5571729165210"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p5571729165210"></a>"alarm_level": "ERROR"</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722920526"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722920526"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722920526"></a>},</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p357182925213"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p357182925213"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p357182925213"></a>"detector_info": [</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35702955218"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35702955218"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35702955218"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722917529"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722917529"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p105722917529"></a>"detector_name": "EsdTestDetector",</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10577296521"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10577296521"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p10577296521"></a>"metric_name": "openGauss_blks_hit_ratio",</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p757729185213"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p757729185213"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p757729185213"></a>"detector_kwargs": {</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35782995211"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35782995211"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p35782995211"></a>"alpha": 0.05</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p2058132914523"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p2058132914523"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p2058132914523"></a>}</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6586294524"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6586294524"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6586294524"></a>}</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p358102919523"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p358102919523"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p358102919523"></a>]</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6581229185217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6581229185217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p6581229185217"></a>}</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p295310818718"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p295310818718"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p295310818718"></a>For the optional range of detection algorithms and the corresponding detection parameters under the detector_info field, see the description below the table.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p20953178575"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p20953178575"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p20953178575"></a>Returns the add status:</p>
<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul6371184014346"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul6371184014346"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul6371184014346"><li>On success, returns Success: add {detector name} for {database instance IP}.</li><li>On failure, returns Failed: add {detector name} for {database instance IP}.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109539812716"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109539812716"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109539812716"></a>{"data":"Success: add detector for IP","success":true}</p></td>
</tr>

</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row1295415813718"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p9954081878"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p9954081878"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p9954081878"></a>/v1/api/app/anomaly-detection/detectors/all/rebuild</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p169541786720"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p169541786720"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p169541786720"></a>PUT</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109544816712"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109544816712"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p109544816712"></a>Rebuilds all anomaly detection detectors based on the backend fallback information.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1277342014112"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1277342014112"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1277342014112"></a>None</p></td>

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139548812712"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139548812712"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p139548812712"></a>{"data":"Success: rebuild detectors for IP","success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row129541481371"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p8954158271"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p8954158271"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p8954158271"></a>/v1/api/app/workload-collection</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p895418810717"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p895418810717"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p895418810717"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p49541981071"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p49541981071"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p49541981071"></a>Obtains the database workload data.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul3270732142017"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul3270732142017"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul3270732142017"><li>param data_source: Required. Data source. Value range: ('asp', 'dbe_perf.statement_history', 'pg_stat_activity').</li><li>param databases: Optional. Database list.</li><li>param schemas: Optional. Schema list.</li><li>param start_time: Optional. Start time.</li><li>param end_time: Optional. End time. Applicable only to asp and dbe_perf.statement_history.</li><li>param db_users: Optional. Database user list.</li><li>param sql_types: Optional. SQL statement type. Value range: ('SELECT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'INSERT').</li><li>param template_id: Optional. SQL template ID.</li><li>param duration: Optional. Execution time of the SQL statement, in ms. Applicable only to dbe_perf.statement_history and pg_stat_activity.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p179553820716"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p179553820716"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p179553820716"></a>Returns workload information.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="24.310000000000002%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1795511814720"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1795511814720"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p1795511814720"></a>{"data":{"header":["user_name","db_name","schema_name","application_name","unique_query_id","start_time","finish_time","duration","n_returned_rows","n_tuples_fetched","n_tuples_returned","n_tuples_inserted","n_tuples_updated","n_tuples_deleted","n_blocks_fetched","n_blocks_hit","n_soft_parse","n_hard_parse","db_time","cpu_time","parse_time","plan_time","data_io_time","lock_wait_time","lwlock_wait_time","query"],"rows":[["user1","db1","public"...]...]},"success":true}</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note1191155715620"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note1191155715620"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_note1191155715620"></a><span class="notetitle"> Note: </span><div class="notebody"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul534018154257"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul534018154257"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_ul534018154257"><li>Explanation of the data_source parameter: asp indicates sampled data, dbe_perf.statement_history indicates historical slow SQL data (the slow SQL threshold is specified by the user), and pg_stat_activity indicates real-time SQL data.</li><li>To avoid API timeout, the maximum amount of returned data is 1000 records.</li></ul>
</div></div></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_row7955185720"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="9.28%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89559818715"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89559818715"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p89559818715"></a>/v1/api/summary/metrics</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199553820713"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199553820713"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p199553820713"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="28.84%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11955198679"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11955198679"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p11955198679"></a>Obtains the list of all metrics.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18.01%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16955585710"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16955585710"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p16955585710"></a>None</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.559999999999999%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3955781279"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3955781279"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_p3955781279"></a>Returns the list of all metrics on the TSDB.</p></td>

Specified time range.</li><li>param from_timestamp: Optional. Start time timestamp. Applicable when latest_minutes is empty.</li><li>param to_timestamp: Optional. End time timestamp. Applicable when latest_minutes is empty.</li><li>param step: Optional. Time interval for fetching data, in milliseconds<span id="ph124621377199"><a name="ph124621377199"></a><a name="ph124621377199"></a>, a positive integer</span><span id="ph17130105192119"><a name="ph17130105192119"></a><a name="ph17130105192119"></a>. Prometheus requires that a time-series sequence in the query result contain no more than 11000 points. If the value selected for this parameter is too small relative to the time range, this error may be reported.</span>.</li><li>param fetch_all: Optional. Whether to fetch all sequences or fetch only once.</li><li>param regex: Optional. When instance is not empty, filters instances using a regular expression.</li><li>param labels: Optional. </li><li>param regex_labels: Optional. Filters sequences using a regular expression. Maximum memory usage of the recommended indexes.</li><li>param sqls: Required. SQL statement list.</li></ul></td>

</table>

>[!NOTE]Note
>
>When calling the /v1/api/app/anomaly-detection/detectors/\{name\} API and using the PUT method to add or modify an anomaly detector, the detector information must be passed in as JSON. The parameter ranges of the detector\_name detection algorithm and the corresponding detector\_kwargs detection algorithm hyperparameter fields under the detecor\_info field are described as follows:
>
>- Studentized residual detector - EsdTestDetector: \{"alpha":0.05\}.
>- Gradient detector - GradientDetector: \{"max\_coef":1,"side":\["positive",\["positive","negative","both"\]\]\}.
>- Monotonic trend detector - IncreaseDetector: \{"alpha":null,"side":\["positive",\["positive","negative","both"\]\]\}.
>- Interquartile range detector - InterQuartileRangeDetector: \{"outliers":\[3,3\]\}.
>- Level shift detector - LevelShiftDetector: \{"agg":\["median",\["median","mean","std"\]\],"outliers":\[null,6\], "side":\["both",\["positive","negative","both"\]\],"window":5\}.
>- Quantile detector - QuantileDetector: \{"high":1,"low":0\}.
>- Seasonal detector - SeasonalDetector: \{"high\_ac\_threshold":0.1,"min\_seasonal\_freq":2,"outliers":\[null,3\], "period":null,"side":\["positive,\["positive","negative","both"\]\],"window":10\}".
>- Spike detector - SpikeDetector: \{"agg":\["median",\["median","mean","std"\]\],"outliers":\[null,3\], "side":\["both",\["positive","negative","both"\]\],"window":1\}.
>- Threshold detector - ThresholdDetector: \{"high":null,"low":null,"percentage":\[null,\[0,1\]\]\}.
>- Volatility shift detector - VolatilityShiftDetector: \{"agg":\["std",\["median","mean","std"\]\],"outliers":\[null,6\], "side":\["both",\["positive","negative","both"\]\],"window":10\}.
>In addition, the detector information JSON also contains the alarm\_info alarm information field. The two subfields, alarm\_type alarm type and alarm\_level alarm severity, are described as follows. When using the /v1/api/summary/alarms API or the /v1/api/summary/alarms/count API to query the details or count of persisted anomaly alarm results, you can use these two parameter fields to further filter the results:
>- All levels of the alarm\_level parameter and their corresponding weights: CRITICAL, FATAL: 50, ERROR: 40, WARNING, WARN: 30, INFO, NOTICE: 20, DEBUG: 10, NOTSET: 0.
>- All types of the alarm\_type parameter: SYSTEM, SLOW\_QUERY, ALARM\_LOG, ALARM, SECURITY, PERFORMANCE.
>When querying anomaly alarms, you can also filter the anomaly type by using the anomaly\_type field.

## Microservice Mode APIs<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_section95413411554"></a>

After DBMind is started in microservice mode, it has the following characteristics:

- Unlike the full-service mode, the microservice mode does not require user management. Therefore, identity authentication and token authentication are no longer required when DBMind APIs are used.
- Because identity authentication is not required, two-way HTTPS authentication is mandatory in this mode to ensure service and data security.
- Because identity authentication and user management are not required, the instance information is a required parameter for all APIs in this mode. The corresponding task is executed on the corresponding instance based on this parameter.

Given the preceding characteristics, even if the API functions are the same, the APIs in microservice mode are separately exposed and are not interoperable with those in full-service mode. They can be called only when DBMind is started in microservice mode. The APIs in the two modes are distinguished by the API route version prefix: the full-service mode uses the v1 prefix, and the microservice mode uses the v2 prefix. The HTTPS APIs provided are as follows:

**Table 2**  DBMind microservice RESTful API list

<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_table20367737161212"></a>

<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row1836819373123"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9368737101215"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9368737101215"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9368737101215"></a>/v2/api/app/slow-sql-rca</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368153731213"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368153731213"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368153731213"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336812372122"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336812372122"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336812372122"></a>Obtains the root cause analysis result of slow SQL.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul14506135918533"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul14506135918533"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul14506135918533"><li>param query: Required. SQL query statement. The string length must be less than 10240.</li><li>param db_name: Required. Database name.</li><li>param schema_name: Optional. Schema name. Default value: public.</li><li>param start_time: Optional. 13-digit timestamp. Recommended. Start time.</li><li>param finish_time: Optional. 13-digit timestamp. Recommended. End time.</li><li>param template_id: Optional. Recommended. Normalized SQL ID.</li><li>param debug_query_id: Optional. Recommended. Unique SQL ID.</li><li>param n_soft_parse: Optional. Number of soft parses. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_hard_parse: Optional. Number of hard parses. Default value: 0.</li><li>param query_plan: Optional. Execution plan.</li><li>param n_returned_rows: Optional. Number of tuples in the result. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_tuples_fetched: Optional. Number of randomly scanned rows. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_tuples_returned: Optional. Number of sequentially scanned rows. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_tuples_inserted: Optional. Number of inserted rows. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_tuples_updated: Optional. Number of updated rows. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_tuples_deleted: Optional. Number of deleted rows. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_blocks_fetched: Optional. Number of buffer block accesses. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_blocks_hit: Optional. Number of buffer block hits. Default value: 0.</li><li>param db_time: Optional. Effective DB time spent, in microseconds. Default value: 0.</li><li>param cpu_time: Optional. CPU time, in microseconds. Default value: 0.</li><li>param parse_time: Optional. Parse time, in microseconds. Default value: 0.</li><li>param plan_time: Optional. Execution time, in microseconds. Default value: 0.</li><li>param data_io_time: Optional. I/O time, in microseconds. Default value: 0.</li><li>param hash_spill_count: Optional. Number of file writes if a spill to disk during hashing. Default value: 0.</li><li>param sort_spill_count: Optional. Number of file writes if a fallback occurs during sorting. Default value: 0.</li><li>param n_calls: Required. Number of calls. Default value: 1.</li><li>param lock_wait_time: Optional. Lock wait time. Default value: 0.</li><li>param lwlock_wait_time: Optional. Lightweight lock time. Default value: 0.</li><li>param tz: Optional. Recommended if there is a time zone difference. Time zone information of the slow SQL. Only the UTC standard is supported.</li><li>A JSON structure must be passed in to connect to the database:</li></ul>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p17654185182417"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p17654185182417"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p17654185182417"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1865416514243"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1865416514243"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1865416514243"></a>'host': Required. IP address of the database on which the slow SQL diagnosis task is to be executed,</p>

<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136546516245"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136546516245"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136546516245"></a>'user': Required. User name of the database on which the slow SQL diagnosis task is to be executed,</p>

<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1565465102420"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1565465102420"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1565465102420"></a>}</p></td>

<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row1336833731211"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p20368173711126"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p20368173711126"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p20368173711126"></a>/v2/api/app/correlation</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p536823711212"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p536823711212"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p536823711212"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p236883781217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p236883781217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p236883781217"></a>Obtains the correlation analysis result of performance metrics.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul136331515125410"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul136331515125410"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul136331515125410"><li>param metric_name: Required. Metric name.</li><li>param instance: Required. Instance address.</li><li>param start_time: Required. Timestamp of the start time. Selects the metric values after this timestamp.</li><li>param end_time: Required. Timestamp of the end time. Selects the metric values before this timestamp. start_time and end_time are two independent parameters rather than a selected time window, so start_time is not required to be earlier than end_time.</li><li>param metric_filter: Optional. Labels of the metric. Supports the following two formats:<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul137412274519"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul137412274519"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul137412274519"><li>The "k1=v1,k2=v2" kv pairs format.</li><li>The "{'k1':'v1','k2':'v2'}" JSON string format.</li></ul>
</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p126451919105"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p126451919105"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p126451919105"></a>Returns the anomaly-correlated metrics and their time-series data.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14283132815019"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14283132815019"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14283132815019"></a>{"data":{"os_mem_usage from ip":[["gs_total_memory_detail_mbytes{\"nodename\": \"dn_6001_6002\", \"type\": \"dynamic_used_memory\"} from ip",1.0,0,[0.32950820234487...]]...]},"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row13681237161217"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p113681737191211"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p113681737191211"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p113681737191211"></a>/v2/api/app/risk-analysis/{metric}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p736843716125"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p736843716125"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p736843716125"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368337191212"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368337191212"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368337191212"></a>Obtains the risk analysis result.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul117142618547"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul117142618547"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul117142618547"><li>param metric: Required. Metric name.</li><li>param instance: Required. Instance address.</li><li>param warning_hours: Optional. Risk analysis duration. Default value: 1.</li><li>param upper: Optional. Upper limit of the metric.</li><li>param lower: Optional. Lower limit of the metric.</li><li>param labels: Optional. Labels of the metric. Supports the following two formats:<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul874413277514"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul874413277514"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul874413277514"><li>The "k1=v1,k2=v2" kv pairs format.</li><li>The "{'k1':'v1','k2':'v2'}" JSON string format.</li></ul>
</li><li>param tz: Optional. Time zone parameter. Only the UTC standard is supported.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1898010291134"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1898010291134"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1898010291134"></a>Returns the future risk analysis result of the current metric.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11996113641010"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11996113641010"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11996113641010"></a>{"data": {"os_disk_usage": [{"abnormal_detail": "No risk identified.","forecast_timestamps": null,"forecast_values": null,"labels": {"device": "/dev/sdd1","from_instance": "ip","from_job": "node_exporter","fstype": "xfs","instance": "ip","job": "reprocessing_exporter","mountpoint": "/data3"},"timestamps": [1733382426553553...],"values": [0.6772665517200114...]}]},"success": true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row0368113716124"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368133781220"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368133781220"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8368133781220"></a>/v2/api/app/workload-collection</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p336814371126"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p336814371126"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p336814371126"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p173684375122"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p173684375122"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p173684375122"></a>Obtains database workload data.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1951619383540"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1951619383540"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1951619383540"><li>param data_source: Required. Data source. Value range: ('asp', 'dbe_perf.statement_history', 'pg_stat_activity').</li><li>param databases: Optional. Database list.</li><li>param schemas: Optional. Schema list.</li><li>param start_time: Optional. Start time.</li><li>param end_time: Optional. End time. Applicable only to asp and dbe_perf.statement_history.</li><li>param db_users: Optional. Database user list.</li><li>param sql_types: Optional. SQL statement type. Value range: ('SELECT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'INSERT').</li><li>param template_id: Optional. SQL template ID.</li><li>param duration: Optional. Execution time of the SQL statement, in ms. Applicable only to dbe_perf.statement_history and pg_stat_activity. Default value: 60.</li></ul>
<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul114119426017"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul114119426017"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul114119426017"><li>A JSON structure must be passed in to connect to the database:</li></ul>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p121411842501"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p121411842501"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p121411842501"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p151411942509"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p151411942509"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p151411942509"></a>'host': Required. IP address of the database on which workload collection is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p3141142206"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p3141142206"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p3141142206"></a>'port': Required. Port of the database on which workload collection is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2141842509"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2141842509"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2141842509"></a>'user': Required. Database user name for workload collection,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p61412427017"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p61412427017"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p61412427017"></a>'pwd': Required. Password of the database user for workload collection</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1214117421402"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1214117421402"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1214117421402"></a>}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p16368037161211"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p16368037161211"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p16368037161211"></a>Returns workload information.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10424151819393"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10424151819393"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10424151819393"></a>{"data":{"header":["user_name","db_name","schema_name","application_name","unique_query_id","start_time","finish_time","duration","n_returned_rows","n_tuples_fetched","n_tuples_returned","n_tuples_inserted","n_tuples_updated","n_tuples_deleted","n_blocks_fetched","n_blocks_hit","n_soft_parse","n_hard_parse","db_time","cpu_time","parse_time","plan_time","data_io_time","lock_wait_time","lwlock_wait_time","query"],"rows":[["user1","db1","public"...]...]},"success":true}</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note16424121853911"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note16424121853911"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note16424121853911"></a><span class="notetitle">Note: </span><div class="notebody"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1239055019157"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1239055019157"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1239055019157"><li>Explanation of the data_source parameter: asp indicates sampled data, dbe_perf.statement_history indicates historical slow SQL data (the slow SQL threshold is specified by the user), and pg_stat_activity indicates real-time SQL data.</li><li>To avoid API timeout, the maximum amount of returned data is 1000 records.</li></ul>
</div></div></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row1436823716126"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936873711121"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936873711121"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936873711121"></a>/v2/api/summary/alarms</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136863720122"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136863720122"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p136863720122"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368163701217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368163701217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1368163701217"></a>Obtains the historical alarm list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul133480115511"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul133480115511"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul133480115511"><li>param pagesize: Optional. Items per page. Recommended. Default value: 20.</li><li>param current: Optional. Current page. Recommended. Default value: 0.</li><li>param instance: Required. Alarm's associated instance. If not passed, returns the alarms within the current cluster.</li><li>param alarm_type: Optional. Alarm type. The optional value range is ['SYSTEM', 'SLOW_QUERY', 'ALARM_LOG', 'ALARM', 'SECURITY', 'PERFORMANCE'].</li><li>param alarm_level: Optional. Alarm severity. The optional value range is ['CRITICAL', 'FATAL', 'ERROR', 'WARNING', 'WARN', 'INFO', 'NOTICE', 'DEBUG', 'NOTSET'].</li><li>param metric_name: Optional. Metric name.</li><li>param start_at: Optional. Query start timestamp. Recommended.</li><li>param end_at: Optional. Query end timestamp. Recommended.</li><li>param anomaly_type: Optional. Anomaly type.</li><li>param group: Optional. Whether to base on alarm type and content classification to group alarms. Default value: False.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1936919373123"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1936919373123"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1936919373123"></a>Returns the historical alarm list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p636916375127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p636916375127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p636916375127"></a>{"data":{"header":["history_alarm_id","instance","metric_name","metric_filter","alarm_type","alarm_level","start_at","end_at","alarm_content","extra_info","anomaly_type","alarm_cause"],"rows":[[65,"ip","os_mem_usage",null,"SYSTEM",30,1684762097001,1684762547001,"mem_usage_spike_detector:Find obvious spikes in memory usage.",null,"Spike"]]},"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row1369133710127"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163691137141213"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163691137141213"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163691137141213"></a>/v2/api/summary/alarms/count</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369103713127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369103713127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369103713127"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369133731214"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369133731214"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369133731214"></a>Obtains the historical alarm count.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul645174165512"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul645174165512"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul645174165512"><li>param instance: Required. Alarm's associated instance. If not passed, returns the alarms within the current cluster.</li><li>param alarm_type: Optional. Alarm type. The optional value range is ['SYSTEM', 'SLOW_QUERY', 'ALARM_LOG', 'ALARM', 'SECURITY', 'PERFORMANCE'].</li><li>param alarm_level: Optional. Alarm severity. The optional value range is ['CRITICAL', 'FATAL', 'ERROR', 'WARNING', 'WARN', 'INFO', 'NOTICE', 'DEBUG', 'NOTSET'].</li><li>param metric_name: Optional. Metric name.</li><li>param start_at: Optional. Query start timestamp. Must not be later than end_at. Recommended.</li><li>param end_at: Optional. Query end timestamp. Must not be earlier than start_at. Recommended.</li><li>param anomaly_type: Optional. Anomaly type.</li><li>param group: Optional. Whether to base on alarm type and content classification to group alarms.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9369123751216"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9369123751216"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p9369123751216"></a>Returns the number of historical alarms.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p57258284115"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p57258284115"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p57258284115"></a>{"data":30,"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row53696378128"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936919377127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936919377127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p936919377127"></a>/v2/api/summary/cluster-diagnosis</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p836913711215"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p836913711215"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p836913711215"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336933718127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336933718127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1336933718127"></a>Obtains the historical cluster diagnosis list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1047820835516"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1047820835516"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1047820835516"><li>param pagesize: Optional. Number of items per page. Recommended. Default value: 20.</li><li>param current: Optional. Current page. Recommended. Default value: 0.</li><li>param instance: Required. Instance IP. If not passed, returns the diagnosis results within the current cluster.</li><li>param start_at: Optional. Query start timestamp. Only results whose timestamp is greater than or equal to this timestamp are returned. Recommended.</li><li>param end_at: Optional. Query end timestamp. Only results whose timestamp is less than or equal to this timestamp are returned. Recommended. If neither start_at nor end_at is passed, no filtering is applied to the timestamp column, and results with all timestamps are returned.</li><li>param cluster_role: Optional. Node type. Value range: ['cn', 'dn'].</li><li>param diagnosis_method: Optional. Diagnosis algorithm. Value range: ['tree', 'logical'].</li><li>param alarm_type: Optional. Alarm type. Value range: ['SYSTEM', 'SLOW_QUERY', 'ALARM_LOG', 'ALARM', 'SECURITY', 'PERFORMANCE'].</li><li>param alarm_level: Optional. Alarm severity. Value range: ['CRITICAL', 'FATAL', 'ERROR', 'WARNING', 'WARN', 'INFO', 'NOTICE', 'DEBUG', 'NOTSET'].</li><li>param is_normal: Optional. Whether to include data whose diagnosis result is Normal. Default value: True.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p23691137121210"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p23691137121210"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p23691137121210"></a>Returns the historical cluster diagnosis list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7430182413387"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7430182413387"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7430182413387"></a>{"data":{"header":["diagnosis_id","instance","timestamp","cluster_role","diagnosis_method","cluster_feature","diagnosis_result","status_code","alarm_type","alarm_level"],"rows":[[1,ip,1695110107087,"cn","logical","{\"ping\": 0, \"cn_status\": 0, \"bind_ip_failed\": 0, \"panic\": 0, \"ffic_updated\": 0, \"cms_heartbeat_restart\": 0, \"cms_phonydead_restart\": 0, \"cn_dn_disconnected\": 0, \"cn_down_to_delete\": 0, \"cn_restart_time_exceed\": 0, \"cn_read_only\": 0, \"cn_restart\": 0, \"cn_start\": 0, \"cn_manual_stop\": 0, \"cn_disk_damage\": 0, \"cn_nic_down\": 0, \"cn_port_conflict\": 0}","Normal",-1,"ALARM",0]]},"success":true}</p>
<div class="note" id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note171145024112"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note171145024112"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_note171145024112"></a><span class="notetitle"> Note: </span><div class="notebody"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul7430192433815"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul7430192433815"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul7430192433815"><li>The timestamps recorded in the timestamp column of the returned result are based on the time of the server where DBMind is deployed. If the time of the server where the metadata database resides is inconsistent with the time of the server where DBMind is deployed, the timestamp results displayed in the metadata database are inconsistent with the actual time of the metadata database server.</li><li>The returned results are sorted according to the following two rules: <a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul164301224123819"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul164301224123819"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul164301224123819"><li>Sorted by timestamp in ascending order.</li><li>When the timestamps are the same, sorted by diagnosis_id in ascending order.</li></ul>
</li></ul>
</div></div>
</td>
</tr>

<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row936933710124"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15369173710127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15369173710127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15369173710127"></a>/v2/api/summary/cluster-diagnosis/count</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p93691437171215"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p93691437171215"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p93691437171215"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14369193712127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14369193712127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14369193712127"></a>Obtains the number of historical cluster diagnoses.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul4419141554"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul4419141554"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul4419141554"><li>param instance: Optional. Instance IP. If not passed, returns the diagnosis results within the current cluster.</li><li>param start_at: Optional. Recommended. Query start timestamp. Only results whose timestamp is greater than or equal to this timestamp are returned. Recommended.</li><li>param end_at: Optional. Recommended. Query end timestamp. Only results whose timestamp is less than or equal to this timestamp are returned. Recommended. If neither start_at nor end_at is passed, no filtering is applied to the timestamp column, and results with all timestamps are returned.</li><li>param cluster_role: Optional. Node type. Value range: ['cn', 'dn'].</li><li>param diagnosis_method: Optional. Diagnosis algorithm. Value range: ['tree', 'logical'].</li><li>param alarm_type: Optional. Alarm type. Value range: ['SYSTEM', 'SLOW_QUERY', 'ALARM_LOG', 'ALARM', 'SECURITY', 'PERFORMANCE'].</li><li>param alarm_level: Optional. Alarm severity. Value range: ['CRITICAL', 'FATAL', 'ERROR', 'WARNING', 'WARN', 'INFO', 'NOTICE', 'DEBUG', 'NOTSET'].</li><li>param is_normal: Optional. Whether to include data whose diagnosis result is Normal. Default value: True.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369437181216"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369437181216"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p7369437181216"></a>Returns the number of historical cluster diagnoses.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1536912375121"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1536912375121"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1536912375121"></a>{"data":30,"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row3369193771216"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1236913710124"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1236913710124"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1236913710124"></a>/v2/api/app/index-recommendation</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p43691937161219"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p43691937161219"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p43691937161219"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13369173717124"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13369173717124"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13369173717124"></a>Obtains the recommended index list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul6141174175510"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul6141174175510"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul6141174175510"><li>param database: Required. Database name.</li><li>param max_index_num: Optional. Maximum number of recommended indexes.</li><li>param max_index_storage: Optional. Maximum memory usage of recommended indexes.</li></ul>
<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1516913248612"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1516913248612"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1516913248612"><li>A JSON structure must be passed in to connect to the database and pass in the workload for which indexes are recommended:</li></ul>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p716917241462"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p716917241462"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p716917241462"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13169152415614"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13169152415614"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p13169152415614"></a>'host': Required. IP address of the database on which index recommendation is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4169324762"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4169324762"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4169324762"></a>'port': Required. Port of the database on which index recommendation is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1216911242615"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1216911242615"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1216911242615"></a>'user': Required. User name of the database on which index recommendation is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p131691224767"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p131691224767"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p131691224767"></a>'pwd': Required. Password of the database user on which index recommendation is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p114151353277"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p114151353277"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p114151353277"></a>'sqls': Required. List of workloads on which index recommendation is to be performed</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1816972420613"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1816972420613"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1816972420613"></a>}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p63692037121210"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p63692037121210"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p63692037121210"></a>Returns the list of recommended indexes.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10369737111215"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10369737111215"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p10369737111215"></a>{"data":[{"advise_indexes":[],"redundant_indexes":[],"total":0,"useless_indexes":[{"columns":"c1","schemaName":"public","statement":"DROP INDEX t1_c1_idx;","tbName":"t1","type":3},{"columns":"c2","schemaName":"public","statement":"DROP INDEX t2_c2_idx;","tbName":"t2","type":3}]},{}],"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row43699374123"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p436923741216"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p436923741216"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p436923741216"></a>/v2/api/summary/database-list</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p53691037151219"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p53691037151219"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p53691037151219"></a>GET</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369193713129"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369193713129"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18369193713129"></a>Obtains the database list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul36159136126"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul36159136126"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul36159136126"><li>param instance: Required. Instance for which the database list is to be obtained.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p637017376125"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p637017376125"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p637017376125"></a>Returns the database list.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p837073761217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p837073761217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p837073761217"></a>{"data":["db1","db2","db3","db4","db5"...],"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row4370737161219"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1337013715124"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1337013715124"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1337013715124"></a>/v2/api/app/metric-diagnosis</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370153731219"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370153731219"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370153731219"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18370173714128"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18370173714128"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18370173714128"></a>Performs metric anomaly diagnosis.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul13112103515484"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul13112103515484"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul13112103515484"><li>param metric_name: Required. Metric name. Value range: os_cpu_user_usage, pg_thread_pool_rate, os_mem_usage, os_disk_usage, mount_usage, os_disk_await, xlog_margin, pg_long_transaction_count.</li><li>param metric_filter: Required. Metric filter. Supports the following two formats:<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul283112781511"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul283112781511"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul283112781511"><li>The "k1=v1,k2=v2" kv pairs format.</li><li>The "{'k1':'v1','k2':'v2'}" JSON string format.</li></ul>
</li><li>param alarm_cause: Required. Analysis method. Value range: high_cpu_usage, high_thread_pool_rate, high_dynamic_mem_usage, high_shared_mem_usage, high_disk_usage, high_io_delay, mem_leak, high_xlog_count, long_transaction.</li><li>param start: Required. Start timestamp of the metric to be analyzed, in milliseconds.</li><li>param end: Required. End timestamp of the metric to be analyzed, in milliseconds.</li></ul></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1737083731212"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1737083731212"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1737083731212"></a>Returns the root cause of the metric anomaly.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1237017373127"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1237017373127"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1237017373127"></a>{"data":{[{'reason1': 0.0, 'reason2': 1.0}, 'conclusion', 'advice']},"success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row737023719124"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163701637101217"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163701637101217"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p163701637101217"></a>/v2/api/summary/sql-trace</p></td>

Query start time.</li><li>param to_timestamp: Optional. Query end time.</li><li>param tz: Optional. Time zone parameter. Only the UTC standard is supported.</li></ul></td>
