<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul15700124311424"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul15700124311424"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul15700124311424"><li>param metric_name: Required. Metric name. Value range: os_cpu_user_usage, pg_thread_pool_rate, os_mem_usage, os_disk_usage, mount_usage, os_disk_await, xlog_margin, pg_long_transaction_count.</li><li>param metric_filter: Required. Metric filter. Supports the following two formats:<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1474071018519"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1474071018519"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1474071018519"><li>KV pairs format "k1=v1,k2=v2";</li><li>JSON string format "{'k1':'v1','k2':'v2'}".</li></ul>
</li><li>param alarm_cause: Required. Analysis method. Value range: high_cpu_usage, high_thread_pool_rate, high_dynamic_mem_usage, high_shared_mem_usage, high_disk_usage, high_io_delay, mem_leak, high_xlog_count, long_transaction.</li><li>param start: Required. Start timestamp of the metric to be analyzed, in milliseconds.</li><li>param end: Required. End timestamp of the metric to be analyzed, in milliseconds.</li></ul>
<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul161589125311"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul161589125311"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul161589125311"><li>A JSON structure must be passed in to connect to the database:</li></ul>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11518911535"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11518911535"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p11518911535"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1215159205317"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1215159205317"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1215159205317"></a>'host': Required. IP address of the database on which metric anomaly diagnosis is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p191511911537"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p191511911537"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p191511911537"></a>'port': Required. Port of the database on which metric anomaly diagnosis is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p615199165312"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p615199165312"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p615199165312"></a>'user': Required. User name of the database on which metric anomaly diagnosis is to be performed,</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p101520995316"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p101520995316"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p101520995316"></a>'pwd': Required. Password of the database user on which metric anomaly diagnosis is to be performed</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p141569175315"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p141569175315"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p141569175315"></a>}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15370143711214"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15370143711214"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p15370143711214"></a>Returns additional information about the root cause of the metric anomaly.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4481825105113"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4481825105113"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p4481825105113"></a>{"data":{"insight_1":[{"k1":v1,"k2":v2,...},{"k1":v1,"k2":v2,...},...],"insight_2":[{"k1":v1,"k2":v2,...},{"k1":v1,"k2":v2,...},...]},..."success":true}</p></td>
</tr>
<tr id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_row13370737141215"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="11.573802541544476%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.1 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2370137131211"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2370137131211"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2370137131211"></a>/v2/api/app/timed-tasks</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="8.484848484848484%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.2 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14370337161210"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14370337161210"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p14370337161210"></a>POST</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.655913978494622%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.3 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370837171213"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370837171213"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p5370837171213"></a>Triggers a specified scheduled task once.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="25.943304007820135%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.4 "><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1863013614594"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1863013614594"></a><ul id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_ul1863013614594"><li>param task_name: Required. Name of the scheduled task to trigger. The optional value range is ['discard_expired_results','anomaly_detection','cluster_diagnose','update_statistics'].</li><li>A JSON structure must be passed in to set the execution parameters of the scheduled task:</li></ul>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p69917476412"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p69917476412"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p69917476412"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1560510531043"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1560510531043"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1560510531043"></a>'cn': When the scheduled task is cluster_diagnose, at least one of the 'dn' field and this field is required. List of CN IPs on which the cluster diagnosis task is to be executed. Default value: empty.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2046212401893"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2046212401893"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p2046212401893"></a>'dn': When the scheduled task is cluster_diagnose, at least one of the 'cn' field and this field is required. List of DN IPs on which the cluster diagnosis task is to be executed. Default value: empty.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p153991575222"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p153991575222"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p153991575222"></a>"detector_name": Required when the scheduled task is anomaly_detection. Name of the anomaly detector. Default value: empty.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p164008715227"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p164008715227"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p164008715227"></a>"duration": Required when the scheduled task is anomaly_detection. Time window, in seconds, over which metrics are captured for anomaly detection. Default value: 600.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p96541438203216"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p96541438203216"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p96541438203216"></a>"alarm_info": Required when the scheduled task is anomaly_detection. Detailed alarm information, which contains four fields.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p342143413210"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p342143413210"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p342143413210"></a>{</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p670911123118"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p670911123118"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p670911123118"></a>"alarm_content": Alarm content.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1390619366310"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1390619366310"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1390619366310"></a>"alarm_type": Alarm type. The optional value range is ['SYSTEM', 'SLOW_QUERY', 'ALARM_LOG', 'ALARM', 'SECURITY', 'PERFORMANCE'].</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p31692327315"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p31692327315"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p31692327315"></a>"alarm_level": Alarm severity. The optional value range is ['CRITICAL', 'FATAL', 'ERROR', 'WARNING', 'WARN', 'INFO', 'NOTICE', 'DEBUG', 'NOTSET'].</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1948813416324"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1948813416324"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1948813416324"></a>"alarm_cause": Alarm cause.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p94002702212"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p94002702212"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p94002702212"></a>},</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1663419159439"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1663419159439"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1663419159439"></a>"detector_info": Required when the scheduled task is anomaly_detection. Detection logic information. A detector can stack multiple detection logic entries, so this is a list value where each item represents one detection logic entry and contains four fields.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p35871414514"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p35871414514"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p35871414514"></a>[{"metric_name": Name of the metric to detect.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p870563718455"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p870563718455"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p870563718455"></a>"detector_name": Detection algorithm. The optional value range is ['GradientDetector', 'IncreaseDetector', 'InterQuartileRangeDetector', 'LevelShiftDetector', 'SeasonalDetector', 'SpikeDetector', 'ThresholdDetector', 'VolatilityShiftDetector', 'QuantileDetector', 'EsdTestDetector', 'ForecastingAnomalyDetector'].</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18521154954519"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18521154954519"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p18521154954519"></a>"metric_filter": Metric label filter condition.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8105136104616"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8105136104616"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p8105136104616"></a>"detector_kwargs": Hyperparameters of the detection algorithm.</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p19950128184619"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p19950128184619"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p19950128184619"></a>}],</p>
<p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p21401511749"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p21401511749"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p21401511749"></a>}</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="13.196480938416421%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.5 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p73715379121"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p73715379121"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p73715379121"></a>Returns the execution status of the scheduled task.</p></td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="27.14565004887585%" headers="mcps1.2.7.1.6 "><p id="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1437103771214"><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1437103771214"></a><a name="zh-cn_topic_0000001667029540_p1437103771214"></a>{"data":{"msg": "The task {task_name} has been successfully executed."}, 'success':true}</p></td>
</tr>

</table>

>[!NOTE]Note
>
>When DBMind starts in microservice mode, it runs statelessly and is unaware of the managed instances. Therefore, when you use the DBMind V2 APIs, the backend still validates the parameters related to instance addresses, but only for IP validity; it cannot verify whether an instance is managed. If the DBMind backend queries and computes metrics by using the instance address provided by the user and the query result is empty, DBMind cannot determine whether the metric is actually empty or the instance address parameter entered by the user is incorrect.

## Metric Unit Query API<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_section9237115619310"></a>

/v1/api/summary/metric-unit/\{metric\} The supported self-developed metric list is shown in [Table 3](#table12110048191610). Metrics collected by some open-source components node\_exporter and Prometheus, and self-developed metrics that are not open for collection in cloud-native mode, are not displayed for now:

## DBMind Scheduled Tasks<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002242300349_section1512644632118"></a>

DBMind embeds scheduled tasks that can be periodically started and automatically executed. You can enable or disable specific scheduled tasks in the DBMind configuration file confpath/dbmind.conf. The scheduled tasks that DBMind already supports and their brief descriptions can be viewed in \[TIMED\_TASK\_LIST\] in dbmind.conf.

- To enable a scheduled task: Find the name of the scheduled task to be enabled in \[TIMED\_TASK\_LIST\] in dbmind.conf, and then add it to the TASK field of \[TIMED\_TASK\] and save. Separate multiple scheduled tasks with ",". For example, to enable the "Cleans up expired data in the metadata database" scheduled task, add "discard\_expired\_results" to the TASK field of \[TIMED\_TASK\].
- To disable a scheduled task: In the TASK field of \[TIMED\_TASK\] in dbmind.conf, delete the scheduled task to be disabled and save.

In the other fields of \[TIMED\_TASK\] in dbmind.conf, you can configure the automatic execution period of each scheduled task. For example, by configuring the value of "discard\_expired\_results\_interval", you can modify the automatic execution period of the "Cleans up expired data in the metadata database" scheduled task accordingly.

After modifying the configuration file, run the following command to make the modification take effect:

```
gs_dbmind service reload -c confpath
```
